# 6.1.2 干擾：含 PCI mod 30 衝突

所屬：[6.1 根因的類型](6-1.md)

## 內文

#### 是什麼、什麼時候會用到

干擾（interference）是有用信號被其他信號蓋過，導致解調失敗、降速或掉話。它和涵蓋不足的差別是：**RSRP 正常，但 SINR 或上行接收品質變差**。來源分三類：自家鄰區（含實體小區識別碼 PCI 規劃不當造成的參考信號序列衝突）、網外的外部干擾源，以及 TDD 網路遇到大氣波導（atmospheric ducting）時的遠端干擾（remote interference，TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 17.1，[TS 38.300](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)）。維運人員常在上行誤塊率升高但涵蓋沒變、新站開台或改 PCI 後鄰區 KPI 劣化、清晨大片 TDD 小區上行同時變差時碰到本節內容。

#### 怎麼做／怎麼判斷

**第一步：先分上行還是下行，再看 RSRP 與 SINR 是否「分家」。**

- 下行：gNB 收集的 SS-RSRP 分佈（量測名 `L1M.SS-RSRP.Bin`，TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.22.1）正常，但 SS-SINR 分佈（`MR.NRScSSSINR.BinX`，clause 5.1.1.32.1）往低端移，是典型的下行干擾或重疊涵蓋特徵。
- 上行：看上行誤塊率、上行干擾量測（多為廠商計數器，名稱依廠商文件）。TS 28.552 附錄 A.104 說明遠端干擾發生時，受害基地台的干擾呈「斜坡」特性：**越靠近保護間隔（gap）的上行符號，干擾越高**。

**第二步：檢查 PCI mod 30（上行參考信號序列群組）。** NR 共有 1008 個 PCI（0～1007），N_ID^cell = 3N_ID^(1) + N_ID^(2)（TS 38.211 V18.10.0 clause 7.4.2.1）。上行有好幾種序列的「序列群組 u」在沒另外設定識別碼時由 PCI 推得（[TS 38.211](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.211/)）：

| 信號 | 序列群組公式（TS 38.211 V18.10.0） | 何時用 PCI |
|---|---|---|
| PUCCH format 0、1、3、4 | u = (f_gh + f_ss) mod 30，f_ss = n_ID mod 30（clause 6.3.2.2.1） | 未設定 `hoppingId` 時 n_ID = N_ID^cell |
| PUSCH DMRS（啟用 transform precoding） | u = (f_gh + n_ID^RS) mod 30（clause 6.4.1.1.1.2） | 未設定 `nPUSCH-Identity` 等條件不成立時 n_ID^RS = N_ID^cell |
| SRS | u = (f_gh + n_ID^SRS) mod 30（clause 6.4.1.4.2） | n_ID^SRS 由 `sequenceId` 給定；在 SRS-Resource IE 中 n_ID^SRS ∈ {0,…,1023}，在 SRS-PosResource-r16 IE 中才為 {0,…,65535}，且不直接取 PCI |

當 `pucch-GroupHopping` 為 'neither'（表示群組跳躍與序列跳躍皆未開），或 PUSCH 未啟用群組與序列跳躍時，f_gh = 0、v = 0，故 u 固定為 n_ID mod 30。**若兩個覆蓋重疊的小區 PCI mod 30 相同，且皆以 PCI 作為 n_ID，且 `pucch-GroupHopping` 為 'neither'，則兩側使用相同的基底序列，導致上行參考信號互相干擾，影響通道估計與解調。若將 `pucch-GroupHopping` 設為 'disable'，則 f_gh = 0，且 v 由以 c_init = 2^5⌊n_ID/30⌋ + (n_ID mod 30) 初始化的偽隨機序列 c(i) 決定，僅在序列長度足夠時才會產生序列跳躍，否則 v = 0；即使 v ≠ 0，也僅保證序列不再固定相同，並不保證兩序列必然不同。** 啟用群組跳躍時，PUSCH 的偽隨機序列以 c_init = ⌊n_ID^RS / 30⌋ 初始化（clause 6.4.1.1.1.2），兩個小區即使 mod 30 相同，只要 ⌊PCI/30⌋ 不同，偽隨機序列就不同，群組跳躍的圖樣也不同。注意：未啟用 transform precoding 的 PUSCH DMRS 用的是以 `scramblingID0`／N_ID^cell 初始化的偽隨機序列（clause 6.4.1.1.1.1），不走 mod 30 這條路。

**第三步：PCI mod 3。** PSS 序列只由 N_ID^(2) 決定，m = (n + 43N_ID^(2)) mod 127（clause 7.4.2.2.1），而 N_ID^(2) 就是 PCI mod 3。mod 3 相同的鄰區 PSS 序列相同，只能靠 SSS 區分。另外 mod 30 相同必定 mod 3 也相同（30 是 3 的倍數）。

**第四步：外部干擾與遠端干擾。** 與話務量無關、全天穩定、集中在某段頻率的底噪抬升，傾向外部干擾，要排頻譜或現場查測；有時段性、多個 TDD 站同時發生、干擾隨上行符號位置遞增，則懷疑大氣波導造成的遠端干擾。TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 17.1 描述 RIM 的無線框架與回程框架；受害 gNB 在確認大氣波導消失後停止發送 RIM-RS。

#### 實際例子

```
工單 INC-20261118-0042（甲電信，虛構）
小區：NR-1207-2，PCI 431，n78 TDD，SCS 30 kHz
現象：11/14 起上行 BLER 6% → 18%（虛構）；客訴「上傳很慢」集中在北側
L1M.SS-RSRP 分佈：與上週相比無明顯變化
MR.NRScSSSINR 中位數：14 dB → 13 dB
異動紀錄：11/13 新站 gNB-1311 開台，NR-1311-1 PCI 461，涵蓋與 NR-1207-2 北側重疊
兩小區組態：pucch-GroupHopping = neither；未設定 hoppingId；未設定 nPUSCH-Identity
上行干擾計數器（廠商）：全天平穩，無時段性，各上行符號間無遞增趨勢
```

逐步判斷：
1. RSRP 沒變、下行 SS-SINR 只掉 1 dB，下行不是主因；問題在上行。
2. 劣化起點（11/14）緊接新站開台（11/13），依 5.5.1 時間軸，這是第一個要檢查的同期變更。
3. 431 mod 30 = 11，461 mod 30 = 11。組態為 'neither' 且沒設 `hoppingId`，依 TS 38.211 clause 6.3.2.2.1（[TS 38.211](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.211/)），兩小區 PUCCH 的 f_gh = 0、f_ss = 11，序列群組 u 都是 11。
4. 干擾計數器沒有時段性、也沒有依符號遞增，與 TS 28.552 Annex A.104 描述的遠端干擾特徵不符，暫不考慮 RIM。

結論：**「PCI mod 30 衝突」是目前最有力的假設，但還不能定案**。還不能下結論的部分：哪些 UE 用了 transform precoding（決定 PUSCH DMRS 是否受影響）、BLER 升高是 PUCCH 還是 PUSCH 為主、北側是否另有外部干擾源。驗證方式可以是經異動流程（第 9 章）改其中一個小區的 PCI，或設定 `hoppingId`／啟用群組跳躍，比對前後 KPI。

#### 容易搞混的地方

1. **PCI mod 30 衝突不是「UE 分不清兩個小區」**。它影響的是上行參考信號序列群組相同造成的互相干擾（TS 38.211 clause 6.3.2.2.1、6.4.1.1.1.2）；UE 辨識小區靠的是由 PSS／SSS 得到的完整 PCI。
2. **mod 30 不是所有上行信號都適用**：SRS 的序列識別碼來自 `sequenceId`；CP-OFDM 的 PUSCH DMRS 不用 mod 30 序列群組；有設定 `hoppingId` 或 `nPUSCH-Identity` 時也不是直接取 PCI。
3. **LTE 與 NR 不可混用**：NR PCI 0～1007，LTE 是 0～503；LTE 的 mod 3 相關機制依 36 系列規格，不能直接套到 NR。
4. **RIM 是 gNB 之間的協調框架，不是單站調參**：TS 38.300 clause 17.1 描述的是受害與施擾 gNB 之間以 RIM 參考信號（RIM-RS）與回程訊息協調的框架，受害 gNB 察覺波導消失後就停止發送 RIM-RS（[TS 38.300](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)）。

#### 和其他小節的關係

「RSRP 正常、SINR 低」要與 6.1.1 的重疊涵蓋區分；PCI 設錯歸 6.1.6。時間軸見 5.5.1，假設與反證見 6.2.1、6.2.2，補收資料見 6.2.3；改 PCI 或跳躍設定屬 6.3.2 需批准的處置，走第 9 章異動流程（量測定義見 [TS 28.552](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)）。

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.211 V18.10.0 clause 6.3.2.2.1、6.4.1.1.1.1、6.4.1.1.1.2、6.4.1.4.2、7.4.2.1、7.4.2.2.1（https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.211/）；3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 17.1（https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/）；3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.22.1、5.1.1.32.1、Annex A.104（https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/）

## 練習題

> 補充練習題（約 105 題）尚未交件，完成後放上。

### GSMA 練習題（86 題）

**1.** What is the term for the interference between UL and DL transmissions in different cells due to atmospheric ducting? [3GPP Release 18]

- A. RIM interference
- B. Backhaul-based interference
- C. CLI interference
- D. Remote interference
- E. Semi-static interference

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

正解：D　（GSMA `teleqna-train-4380`）

</details>

**2.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**4.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.

**5.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**9.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**13.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**17.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**21.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**38.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**51.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**56.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**65.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**78.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.
